# Projection Foundation — generated tables

Generated by `research/projection_foundation/build_report_tables.py` from each sport repo's `research/projection_foundation/results/summary.json`. Bias = mean(projection − actual): positive = projects too high. CRPS is the whole-distribution error (lower is better). Calibration: share of outcomes inside the 80% range (target 80%) and PIT variance ratio (1.00 = calibrated, >1 = ranges too narrow/overconfident, <1 = too wide). Δ columns are game-clustered bootstrap differences; negative = better than the comparator.

## NFL

Splits: **development** 2016-2021 regular seasons (2012-2015 feature warm-up only); **validation** 2022-2023 regular seasons; **holdout** 2024-2025 regular seasons (evaluated once, frozen candidates only); **check_2026** 2026 weeks 1-4, read-only accuracy check (already used in market discovery; never tuned on); **embargo** 2026 weeks >= 5: untouched market holdout, never scored here
  Code: `baac85fac4`, pf_core 1.0.0, generated 2026-10-17T07:49:21Z.

### Data inventory

| Dataset | Seasons | Rows | Point-in-time class | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| nflverse player weekly stats (stats_player) | 2012-2026 | 257,732 | reconstructable | Outcomes and, lagged, the core features. Release available 1999+; 2012-2026 downloaded (2012-2015 warm-up only). 2026 = weeks 1-5 + Thursday week 6. |
| nflverse team weekly stats (stats_team) | 2012-2026 | 7,818 | reconstructable | Team pass attempts / carries / yards per game: team-volume outcomes and opponent 'allowed' histories. |
| schedules with closing lines (games.csv) | 1999-2026 | 7,548 | archived | spread_line / total_line are the final pregame (closing) lines: pregame by construction, but recorded retrospectively (not our own vintage). QB ids are the ACTUAL starter for past games (retrospective; announced pregame in practice) and the projected starter for upcoming games. |
| weather / roof (schedule temp, wind, roof) | 1999-2026 | 0 | retrospective | temp/wind are game-time observations, not forecasts -> not used as features. Roof is pregame-known and used. Timestamped NWS/Open-Meteo forecasts exist only from the 2026 context capture on the market-data branch. |
| snap counts (PFR) | 2012-2026 | 332,075 | reconstructable | 2012+. Used for offense-snap share history and to add zero-stat participation rows. Participation itself is post-game. |
| injury reports (final designation) | 2012-2026 | 78,236 | retrospective | 2009-2026 Out/Doubtful/Questionable + practice status. No per-row timestamp (date_modified in 2024 only), rebuilt in place; treated as NEAR-pregame (Friday report) and flagged. |
| weekly rosters (status ACT/INA/RES) | 2012-2026 | 609,004 | retrospective | Snapshot time unknown; INA is the gameday inactive list (90 min pre-kickoff). Used only for the forward candidate set, never as a backtest feature. |
| depth charts | 2012-2026 | 1,689,644 | retrospective | 2012-2024 weekly NFL depth charts without timestamps (retrospective). 2025+ are ESPN daily snapshots with a 'dt' timestamp (archived). Not used in models (only 2 archived seasons). |
| Next Gen Stats (passing/rushing/receiving) | all | 27,613 | reconstructable | 2016+ weekly aggregates (separation, cushion). Downloaded, not used in these models (not evaluated here). |
| players / rosters / crosswalk | all | 24,854 | reconstructable | IDs, rookie season, draft round (draft round is pregame). gsis<->pfr crosswalk built by nfl_edge/data/ids.py. |
| play-by-play | 1999-2026 | 0 | reconstructable | Not downloaded for this study (team totals from stats_team suffice); available from the pbp release if drive-level pace is needed. |
| pbp participation (routes, personnel) | 2016-2025 | 0 | reconstructable | Not downloaded. research/opportunity found route/role shares improve target and carry projection as extra regressors (7/7 seasons) but not on the Kalshi-listed population (research/listed_validation) — priority candidate. |
| archived pregame incumbent projections (shadow ledger) | 2026 | 2 | archived | Week-1 2026 ledger in this checkout; later weeks on the market-data branch. Not needed: the incumbent was replayed walk-forward with its own code (nfl/incumbent.py). |
| pregame timestamped injury/availability vintages | 2026 only | 0 | archived | Sleeper/ESPN captures on the market-data branch (context-capture.yml) start in 2026: too short for a backtest. Historical seasons have no timestamped vintage -> missing. |

## CFB

Splits: **development** 2015-2019 seasons (2015 = warm-up for E; ladder metrics 2016-2019). 2020 is COVID-anomalous and excluded; **validation** 2021-2022 seasons (model selection); **holdout** 2023-2024 seasons (scored once for the frozen winner, baseline, incumbents, market); **holdout_2025** 2025 season (second holdout, scored once); **live_2026** 2026 weeks played to date (ESPN source; sanity only)
  Code: `94c15a7e48`, pf_core 1.0.0, generated 2026-10-17T07:11:45Z.

### Data inventory

| Dataset | Seasons | Rows | Point-in-time class | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| game results / schedule (FBS-involved; FCS-vs-FCS excluded) | 2014-2026 | 10,829 | archived | CFBD 2014-2025 (research-data cache), ESPN production log 2026. Neutral site, conference game, week. |
| team-game box scores (plays, pass/rush att & yards, completions, turnovers, possession) | 2014-2026 | 21,651 | reconstructable | plays = box rush att + pass att in both sources (sacks are rushes). 2026 ESPN means match 2024-25 CFBD (plays 65.9 vs 66.6, rush yds 151.6 vs 154.4). |
| game-level EPA/play, success rate, explosiveness (all plays and garbage-time filtered) | 2014-2025 | 20,632 | retrospective | CFBD advanced box (its EPA model is fitted retrospectively); used only lagged. 2026: success rate from ESPN drives, NO EPA -> EPA family has no live season. |
| drives / possessions | 2014-2026 | 21,548 | reconstructable | CFBD advanced 'drives'; 2026 from ESPN drive summaries. |
| raw play-by-play | 2014-2026 (remote) | 0 | reconstructable | sportsdataverse espn_cfb_pbp / cfbfastR_cfb_pbp releases are reachable but were NOT downloaded (~100 MB/season); game-level CFBD aggregates already carry EPA/SR with and without garbage time. |
| player box scores (passing, rushing, receiving) | 2014-2026 | 229,727 | reconstructable | sportsdataverse espn_cfb_player_box. NO targets, snaps, routes or positions; only players with a stat appear. |
| FBS/FCS classification and conferences | 2014-2026 | 10,829 | archived | per-game classification from the schedule source (2026: Sacramento State / North Dakota State listed FBS). |
| talent composite (247) | 2015-2026 | 0 | reconstructable | 2014 file empty. Published pre-season; may be revised in-season (low contamination risk). |
| recruiting class points | 2014-2025 | 0 | reconstructable | 2026 class not cached: 2026 uses the 2023-2025 classes (3-year average). |
| returning production (offence PPA/usage) | 2014-2026 | 0 | reconstructable | Offence only (no defensive returning production in the source). |
| transfer portal | 2025 only | 0 | missing | portal.json exists for 2025 only -> not usable for a walk-forward test. |
| coaches (new head coach flag) | 2014-2026 | 0 | reconstructable |  |
| QB changes | 2014-2026 | 0 | reconstructable | derived from player box (leading passer of the previous game vs this game). Injury reports: 2026 current snapshot only (data/football/2026/availability.json) -> no historical injury data. |
| weather / venue | 2014-2025 | 0 | retrospective | research-data-v2enrich weather_archive = OBSERVED weather (contaminated as a pre-game input); weather_hforecast (historical forecasts) exists but was not evaluated here. Venue id in games. |
| closing lines (spread / total) | 2014-2026 | 10,732 | archived | CFBD multi-provider lines 2014-2025 (median across providers; untimestamped, treated as closing), ESPN core odds 2026. Benchmark only - never a model input. |
| SP+ / season advanced ratings | 2014-2025 | 0 | retrospective | end-of-season values; not used. |
| archived pregame forecasts in repo | 2026 | 0 | archived | script-engine frozen artifacts (data/scripting/live/frozen, frozen_v2: claims + historical margin ranges, no probabilities). The retired engine's expected points 2014-2025 (base_repair source contract) and the v2 research walk-forward predictions 2017-2025 are RETROSPECTIVE replays, not archived pre-game. |

## MLB

Splits: **development** pitchers & teams: 2023-2024 regular seasons (2022 = history only); hitters: 2024 (fits use 2024-05-01 on; no 2022-23 player-level hitter lines); **validation** 2025 regular season (model selection); **holdout** 2026 regular season (frozen winners scored once; incumbent comparisons on matched rows)

## NHL

Splits: **development** 2021-22, 2022-23 regular seasons (fit shrinkage, betas, dispersion, stack weights); **validation** 2023-24 regular season (model and distribution selection); **holdout** 2024-25 and 2025-26 regular seasons (evaluated once for frozen candidates) (saves re-scored once after a validation-motivated drift fix; disclosed); **prospective** 2026-27 regular season to date (games 2026020001-2026020120, archived; overlaps PLAYER_SIM_V1)
  Code: `322f533262`, pf_core 1.0.0, generated 2026-10-17T07:38:15Z.

### Data inventory

| Dataset | Seasons | Rows | Point-in-time class | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| skater/goalie box scores (official boxscore) | 2021-22..2026-27 | 240,421 | reconstructable | regular-season games by season 2021-22: 1312, 2022-23: 1312, 2023-24: 1312, 2024-25: 1312, 2025-26: 1312, 2026-27: 120; 2026-27 = 120 games from the data-archive player_events (through 2026-10-16) superseding 3 committed games. Post-game facts used only as history. |
| TOI by strength (EV / PP / SH / EA / EN / OT from shift charts) | 2021-22..2026-27 | 238,372 | reconstructable | share of games with shift charts: 2021: 1.000, 2022: 1.000, 2023: 1.000, 2024: 0.957, 2025: 1.000, 2026: 1.000. 2024-25 lacks shift charts for 57 games (strength split NaN there). |
| line combinations (pairwise shared ice, coice) | 2021-22..2026-27 | 1,489,660 | reconstructable | post-game deployment; the PIT role used for game g is the previous game's lines / PP unit. Tonight's lines are NOT known historically. |
| pregame projected lines / PP units (DailyFaceoff) | 2026-27 only (from 2026-09-30) | 306 | archived | archived hourly snapshots (context/lines); none for 2021-22..2025-26, so the value of confirmed lines cannot be backtested. See lineup_accuracy. |
| starting goalie (actual, boxscore starter flag) | 2021-22..2026-27 | 13,236 | retrospective | saves projections are CONDITIONAL_ON_ACTUAL_STARTER; no PIT starter source exists before 2026-27. |
| pregame starting-goalie observations (DailyFaceoff status) | 2026-27 only | 360 | archived | PROJECTED / PROBABLE / CONFIRMED with observation time; see starter_accuracy. |
| shot events with x/y, type, strength (xG possible) | 2021-22..2026-27 | 783,648 | reconstructable | own logistic xG fitted on development seasons; used as lagged player/team shot quality. |
| schedule, rest days, back-to-backs | 2021-22..2026-27 | 6,680 | reconstructable | game dates are known in advance; derived from the game list. |
| travel distance / time zones | 2021-22..2026-27 | 6,680 | reconstructable | previous venue -> current venue haversine on approximate arena coordinates. |
| injury reports (ESPN) | 2026-27 only | 324 | archived | historical injury status missing; scratches are inferred post hoc. |
| archived prospective forecasts PLAYER_SIM_V1 (predictions_player) | 2026-27 only | 225,485 | archived | goals / assists / points / first-goal / saves ladders + expected TOI, PP TOI, SOG, G, A, P per player snapshot; incumbent on the 2026-27 overlap (last pregame snapshot per player-game). |
| PLAYER_SIM_V1 walk-forward predictions (docs/research/player_sim_v1) | skaters 2025-26; goalies 2024-25, 2025-26 | 52,479 | reconstructable | historical walk-forward (deployment from recent shifts, actual dressed lineup and starters); incumbent on holdout. |
| DATA_ONLY_V2 walk-forward game lambdas (docs/research/walk_forward_v2_*_games.csv) | 2024-25, 2025-26 | 2,624 | reconstructable | SIM2_ST expected home/away goals = team-goals incumbent on holdout (valid pregame arm). |
| dressed lineup / scratches | 2021-22..2026-27 | 240,421 | retrospective | evaluation is conditional on playing (Kalshi settles a non-entering player at fair price). |
